<tr><td><strong>9D002</strong></td><td>“Software” required for the “production” of equipment specified in 9A. or 9B.</td></tr>
<tr><td><strong>9D003</strong></td><td>“Software” required for the “use” of full authority digital electronic engine controls (FADEC) for propulsion systems specified in 9A. or equipment specified in 9B., as follows:<br>a. “Software” in digital electronic controls for propulsion systems, aerospace test facilities or air breathing aero-engine test facilities;<br>b. Fault-tolerant “software” used in FADEC systems for propulsion systems and associated test facilities.</td></tr>
<tr><td><strong>9D004</strong></td><td>Other “software”, as follows:<br>a. “Software”, other than that specified in 2D101, specially designed for vibration test equipment using real time digital controls with individual exciters (thrusters) with a maximum thrust exceeding 100 kN;<br>b. 2D or 3D viscous “software” validated with wind tunnel or flight test data required for detailed engine flow modelling;<br>c. “Software” required for the “development” or “production” of real time full authority electronic test facilities for engines or components specified in 9A.;<br>d. “Software” for testing aero gas turbine engines, assemblies or components, specially designed to collect, reduce and analyse data in real time, and capable of feedback control, including the dynamic adjustment of test articles or test conditions, as the test is in progress;<br>e. “Software” specially designed to control directional solidification or single crystal casting;<br>f. “Software” in “source code”, “object code” or machine code required for the “use” of active compensating systems for rotor blade tip clearance control;<br>NOTE: 9D004.f. does not specify “software” embedded in non-specified equipment or required for maintenance activities associated with the calibration or repair or updates to the active compensating clearance control system.</td></tr>
<tr><td><strong>9D101</strong></td><td>“Software” specially designed for the “use” of goods specified in 9B105, 9B106, 9B116 or 9B117.</td></tr>
<tr><td><strong>9D103</strong></td><td>“Software” specially designed for modelling, simulation or design integration of the systems specified in 9A004 or 9A104, or the subsystems specified in 9A005, 9A007, 9A105.a., 9A106, 9A108, 9A116 or 9A119.<br>NOTE: “Software” specified in 9D103 remains controlled when combined with specially designed hardware specified in 4A102.</td></tr>
<tr><td><strong>9E</strong></td><td><strong>Technology</strong></td></tr>
<tr><td><strong>9E001</strong></td><td>“Technology” according to the General Technology Note for the “development” of equipment or “software” specified in 9A001.c., 9A004 to 9A011, 9B. or 9D.</td></tr>
<tr><td><strong>9E002</strong></td><td>“Technology” according to the General Technology Note for the “production” of equipment specified in 9A001.c., 9A004 to 9A011 or 9B.<br>NOTE: “Development” or “production” “technology” specified in 9E. for gas turbine engines remains controlled when used as “use” technology for repair, rebuild and overhaul.<br>Excluded from control are: technical data, drawings or documentation for maintenance activities directly associated with calibration, removal or replacement of damaged or unserviceable line replaceable units, including replacement of whole engines or engine modules.<br>(For “technology” for the repair of controlled structures, laminates or materials, see 1E002.f.)</td></tr>
<tr><td><strong>9E003</strong></td><td>Other “technology”, as follows:<br>a. “Technology” “required” for the “development” or “production” of the following gas turbine engine components or systems:<br>1. Directionally solidified gas turbine blades, vanes or tip shrouds rated to operate at gas path temperatures exceeding 1,593 K (1,320°C);<br>2. Single crystal blades, vanes or tip shrouds;<br>3. Multiple domed combustors operating at average burner outlet temperatures exceeding 1,643 K (1,370°C), or combustors incorporating thermally decoupled combustion liners, non-metallic liners or non-metallic shells;<br>4. Components manufactured from organic “composite” materials designed to operate above 588 K (315°C), or from metal “matrix” “composite”, ceramic “matrix”, intermetallic or intermetallic reinforced materials specified in 1A002 or 1C007;<br>5. Uncooled turbine blades, vanes, tip-shrouds or other components designed to operate at gas path temperatures of 1,323 K (1,050°C) or more;<br>6. Cooled turbine blades, vanes or tip-shrouds, other than those described in 9E003.a.1. and 2., exposed to gas path temperatures of 1,643 K (1,370°C) or more;<br>7. Airfoil-to-disk blade combinations using solid state joining;<br>8. Gas turbine engine components using “diffusion bonding” “technology” specified in 2E003.b.;<br>9. Damage tolerant gas turbine engine rotating components using powder metallurgy materials specified in 1C002.b.;<br>10. FADEC for gas turbine and combined cycle engines and their related diagnostic components, sensors and specially designed components;<br>11. Adjustable flow path geometry and associated control systems for:<br>a. Gas generator turbines;<br>b. Fan or power turbines;<br>c. Propelling nozzles;<br>NOTES:<br>1. Adjustable flow path geometry and associated control systems do not include inlet guide vanes, variable pitch fans, variable stators or bleed valves for compressors.<br>2. 9E003.a.11. does not control “development” or “production” “technology” for adjustable flow path geometry for reverse thrust.<br>12. Rotor blade tip clearance control systems employing active compensating casing “technology” limited to a design and development data base;<br>13. Gas bearings for gas turbine engine rotor assemblies;<br>14. Wide chord hollow fan blades without part-span support;<br>b. “Technology” “required” for the “development” or “production” of:<br>1. Wind tunnel aero-models equipped with non-intrusive sensors capable of transmitting data from the sensors to the data acquisition system;<br>2. “Composite” propeller blades or propfans capable of absorbing more than 2,000 kW at flight speeds exceeding Mach 0.55;<br>c. “Technology” “required” for the “development” or “production” of gas turbine engine components using “laser”, water jet or ECM/EDM hole drilling processes to produce holes with:<br>1. a. Depths more than four times their diameter;<br>b. Diameters less than 0.76 mm; and<br>c. Incidence angles equal to or less than 25°; or<br>2. a. Depths more than five times their diameter;<br>b. Diameters less than 0.4 mm; and<br>c. Incidence angles of more than 25°;<br>Technical Note: For the purposes of 9E003.c., incidence angle is measured from a plane tangential to the airfoil surface at the point where the hole axis enters the airfoil surface.<br>d. “Technology” “required” for the “development” or “production” of helicopter power transfer systems or tilt rotor or tilt wing “aircraft” power transfer systems:<br>1. Capable of loss-of-lubrication operation for 30 minutes or more; or<br>2. Having an input power-to-weight ratio equal to or more than 8.87 kW/kg;<br>e. 1. “Technology” for the “development” or “production” of reciprocating diesel engine ground vehicle propulsion systems having all of the following:<br>a. A box volume of 1.2 m<sup>3</sup> or less;<br>b. An overall power output of more than 750 kW based on 80/1269/EEC, ISO 2534 or equivalents; and<br>c. A power density of more than 700 kW/m<sup>3</sup> of box volume;<br>Technical Note: Box volume is the product of three perpendicular dimensions measured in the following way:<br>Length: The length of the crankshaft from front flange to flywheel face;<br>Width: The widest of the following:<br>a. The outside dimension from valve cover to valve cover;<br>b. The dimensions of the outside edges of the cylinder heads; or<br>c. The diameter of the flywheel housing;<br>Height: The largest of the following:<br>a. The dimension of the crankshaft centre-line to the top plane of the valve cover (or cylinder head) plus twice the stroke; or<br>b. The diameter of the flywheel housing.<br>2. “Technology” “required” for the “production” of specially designed components, as follows, for high output diesel engines:<br>a. “Technology” “required” for the “production” of engine systems having all of the following components employing ceramics materials specified in 1C007:<br>1. Cylinder liners;<br>2. Pistons;<br>3. Cylinder heads; and<br>4. One or more other components (including exhaust ports, turbochargers, valve guides, valve assemblies or insulated fuel injectors);<br>b. “Technology” “required” for the “production” of turbocharger systems, with single-stage compressors having all of the following:<br>1. Operating at pressure ratios of 4:1 or higher;<br>2. A mass flow in the range from 30 to 130 kg per minute; and<br>3. Variable flow area capability within the compressor or turbine sections;<br>c. “Technology” “required” for the “production” of fuel injection systems with a specially designed multifuel (e.g., diesel or jet fuel) capability covering a viscosity range from diesel fuel (2.5 cSt at 310.8 K (37.8°C)) down to gasoline fuel (0.5 cSt at 310.8 K (37.8°C)), having both of the following:<br>1. Injection amount in excess of 230 mm<sup>3</sup> per injection per cylinder; and<br>2. Specially designed electronic control features for switching governor characteristics automatically depending on fuel property to provide the same torque characteristics by using the appropriate sensors;<br>3. “Technology” “required” for the “development” or “production” of high output diesel engines for solid, gas phase or liquid film (or combinations thereof) cylinder wall lubrication, permitting operation to temperatures exceeding 723 K (450°C), measured on the cylinder wall at the top limit of travel of the top ring of the piston.<br>Technical Note: High output diesel engines: Diesel engines with a specified brake mean effective pressure of 1.8 MPa or more at a speed of 2,300 r.p.m., provided the rated speed is 2,300 r.p.m. or more.</td></tr>
<tr><td><strong>9E101</strong></td><td>“Technology” according to the General Technology Note for the “development” or “production” of goods specified in 9A101, 9A104 to 9A111 or 9A115 to 9A119.</td></tr>
<tr><td><strong>9E102</strong></td><td>“Technology” according to the General Technology Note for the “use” of goods specified in 9A004 to 9A011, 9A101, 9A104 to 9A111, 9A115 to 9A119, 9B105, 9B106, 9B115, 9B116, 9B117, 9D101 or 9D103.</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>
